user: PLEASE IMPLEMENT THIS PLAN:
user: # Tindahan ni Tinapa

user: ## Summary

user: Create a separate storefront app in a new **private GitHub repository**, `AK-Lmn/pbc-gb`. Display the site name **Tindahan ni Tinapa**. Keep PBC as her main selling site.

user: The storefront will take design cues from the [Niimbot reference](https://budolsupplies.zite.so/on-hand/niimbot), with on-hand stock, group-buy batches, cart, and order tracking. It will send orders and receipt proofs to admin-pbc.

user: ## Implementation

user: - Create the private `AK-Lmn/pbc-gb` repository and deploy it as a separate Vercel project. Use its Vercel URL until a custom domain is chosen.
user: - Build the storefront for on-hand and group-buy products. Support categories, stock counts, sale prices, batch deadlines and statuses, and optional arrival estimates.
user: - Extend admin-pbc’s catalog model and management screens for the new storefront. Keep its catalog separate from PBC’s existing products.
user: - Extend admin-pbc’s order API and dashboard with group-buy filtering and per-order batch and shipment details. Reuse its payment verification and Google Drive receipt flow.
user: - Allow mixed carts. At checkout, customers choose one combined shipment or separate shipments. Apply the existing area-based rate per shipment and show the full amount before payment.
user: - Reduce on-hand stock after payment verification. Calculate prices, stock availability, and shipping fees on the server. Keep existing orders and PBC behavior compatible.

user: ## Tests and acceptance

user: - PBC keeps its existing catalog; Tindahan ni Tinapa shows only its own catalog.
user: - Closed or expired group-buy batches reject new orders.
user: - Mixed-order totals match the chosen shipping option.
user: - Stock changes only after payment verification.
user: - Orders and receipt proofs appear in admin-pbc’s dedicated group-buy view.
user: - Existing orders and order tracking continue to work.

user: ## Assumptions

user: - Group-buy batches have no minimum-quantity target in v1.
user: - Admin changes batch status after its deadline; ordering closes automatically at the deadline.
user: - Separate shipping creates one on-hand parcel and one parcel per group-buy batch.
user: - The new repo is private under the same GitHub owner as PBC and admin-pbc, `AK-Lmn`.
